Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the geophysics framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 721. The resulting operator achieves bound O(185/n^3).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_721(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 721. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^185(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 215.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: For any geophysics module M satisfying the invariance principle hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 75. The projective resolution has length at most 776 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

For the inductive step, we decompose the geophysics structure using the invariance principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 776. Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
